4. Errors and fixes:
   - **패 glyph was structurally wrong**: Initially drawn as ᄑᆡ (ㅍ + 아래아 + ㅣ), which is not "패" but an archaic/wrong syllable. User's eye for accuracy caught this in 2x screenshot. Fixed by redrawn as proper 패 = ㅍ + ㅐ (ㅏ bar with right dot).
   - **건 glyph was unreadable**: Drawn as loose ㄱ + ㅓ (as two separate corners) + ㄴ. Didn't read as "건", looked like scattered geometry. Fixed by repositioning ㅓ stem clearly with left-positioned dot.
   - **화 glyph read ambiguously**: Looked like "호 + ㅏ" instead of unified 화. Fixed by tightening ㅏ positioning to the 호 block and compacting ㅎ ring.
   - **Tile whitespace felt empty**: K-Beauty large tile had lots of negative space. User (correctly) flagged this as either "unintended" or "needs intentional texture". Fixed by adding faint oversized glyph watermark (5% opacity) as background, turning negative space into intentional paper-like texture.
   - **Watermark CSS not rendering in Vercel**: First deployment (e681676) committed, but Vercel build took time. My initial polling (looking for "Hunminjeongeum" string) mistakenly thought it was live when old build was still serving. Fixed by polling for the actual watermark class (opacity-[0.05]) which only exists in new build.
   - **User name confusion**: Initially used "Vision Kim" (from site About page) in LinkedIn message template. User corrected: "나는 vision kim이 아니라 MUBIN KIM이야!!" Then immediately: "mubin kim 말고 내 영어이름 John Kim으로 변경해줘!!" (Change to English name John Kim). This is the pending task.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Design hierarchy under-selling USP**: Original landing talked about government registries but buried the proof under warnings/pills. Solution: moved registry evidence to hero (live mock panel with MFDS→CGMP→NTS checks), created dedicated dark section, made emerald the exclusive "government evidence" color.
   - **Glyphs looking broken rather than archaic**: SVG hand-drawn approach works, but line weight and proportions matter enormously. At 7px stroke, glyphs looked spindly. At 8.5px + 7.5px dots, they read as confident brush marks. Also needed to fix structure (패/건/화 were wrong shapes, not just thin).
   - **Negative space in tiles**: Initially tiles had large empty areas in K-Beauty large card. "Could look unfinished." Solution: watermark approach (faint giant glyph in background) treats negative space as intentional texture (like hanji paper), not mistake.
   - **Deployment verification ambiguity**: Can't just grep for "deployed" or check git log; Vercel's async build means old HTML might still serve. Solution: each change includes a unique CSS class/string only in that change, poll for that exact marker.
   - **Real search validation**: Needed to prove the government registry cross-check actually works end-to-end. Solution: triggered real /api/source route with internal-api-key bypass, sent real query ("Korean collagen ampoule serum OEM"), got back real suppliers (COSMAX, 한국콜마) with verified gov badges + live NTS active-business confirmation.

```
Hi Remzi — thank you for the follow, it's genuinely an honour to connect. Building bridges between producers and global buyers across Metro, Real and now Global24 is exactly the world I care about.

I'm building something similar from the Korea side: GloSource AI helps international buyers shortlist and verify Korean manufacturers — K-beauty, K-food and beyond — cross-checking each candidate against Korea's official government registries (MFDS, HACCP, live tax-office status).

Given Global24's reach with buyers sourcing food and non-food worldwide, I suspect our work is more complementary than competing — your clients occasionally need Korean suppliers, and mine are sometimes asked about Turkey. I'd love to learn how you built Global24's producer network over the years, and explore whether there's a natural way to send good business each other's way.

Warm regards,
John Kim · GloSource AI (glosourceai.com)
```

## 🤖 Claude

**솔직하게 말씀드리면: 4건 중 2건 완료, 2건 미완입니다.** 그리고 방금 점검에서 빠뜨린 부분을 하나 발견해서 마저 처리했습니다.

## 방금 발견·수정한 것
아까 커밋(`27b0112`)에 **unescape 수정 2개 파일만 들어가고, URL 치환된 10개 파일은 커밋이 안 된 상태**였습니다. diff를 전수 확인해서 순수 URL 치환뿐인 것을 검증한 뒤 `8c0a060`으로 커밋 완료했습니다. Python 문법 검사·JSON 파싱 검사도 통과했습니다.

## 핸드오프 4건 현황

| # | 항목 | 상태 |
|---|------|------|
| 1 | 죽은 홍보링크 (`glosource-ai.vercel.app` → `glosourceai.com`) | ✅ 완료 — repo 전체 0건, 커밋 `8c0a060` |
| 2 | YouTube 제목 HTML 엔티티 (`&#39;`) | ✅ 완료 — 두 파일 `html.unescape()` 적용, 커밋 `27b0112` |
| 3 | Morning Brief 중복 + stale 뉴스 | ❌ 미착수 — **Oracle 서버 cron 로그 확인이 필요**해서 이 PC 로컬 수정만으로는 절반밖에 못 고칩니다 (`jarvis_push.py`의 24h 확장 로직은 로컬에서 고칠 수 있음) |
| 4 | Kaggle RAG 임베딩 38일 stale | ❌ 미착수 — Kaggle notebook 수동 재실행 필요 (외부 접근) |

## 참고
- mbkim-planet 커밋은 **로컬 main에만** 있고 origin에는 push 안 했습니다. 로컬 cron이 이 repo를 직접 실행하므로 내일 발행분부터는 바로 정상 링크가 나가지만, push까지 원하시면 말씀해 주세요.
- 3번 중 로컬에서 고칠 수 있는 부분(`jarvis_push.py`의 `_sec_news()` 최신 1건 제한)은 지금 바로 진행 가능합니다. 진행할까요?
